<script type="text/javascript">
            function makeSearch(sender) {
                var $txtSearch = jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_TopSearchBox');

                if (Sys.Extended.UI.TextBoxWrapper.get_Wrapper($get('ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_TopSearchBox'))._isWatermarked) {
                    var txtWatermark = $get('ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_TopSearchBox').value;

                    if ($txtSearch != null && txtWatermark != null && txtWatermark != $txtSearch)
                        return false;
                }

                if ($txtSearch != null) {
                    var keywords = $txtSearch.val();
                    if (keywords != null && keywords.length > 2) {
                        keywords = encodeURIComponent(keywords).replace(/%20/g, '+');
                        var searchNavigateUrl = jQuery(sender).data('navigate-url');
                        if (getSuggestionsRequest != null) {
                            getSuggestionsRequest.abort();                        
                        }

                        searchNavigateUrl += keywords;

                        var searchSongsOnly = jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_chbSearchSongsOnly').attr("checked");
                        var searchDAMOnly = jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_chbSearchDAMOnly').attr("checked");

                        if (searchSongsOnly === "checked") {
                            searchNavigateUrl += "&songsOnly=true";
                        }
                        if (searchDAMOnly == "checked")
                            searchNavigateUrl += "&Show in DAM=True";

                        var categoryId = jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_ddlCategories').val();
                        if (categoryId)
                        {
                            searchNavigateUrl += '&Product category=' + categoryId;
                        }

                        window.location = searchNavigateUrl;

                        return true;
                    }
                }

                return false;
            }
         </script>
<script language="javascript" type="text/javascript">
              var getSuggestionsRequest = null;
                $(function () {
                    var getSuggestionsTimer = null;

                    var updateSuggestionList = function (prefixText) {
                        if (prefixText.length < 2)
                            return;
                        if (jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_chbSearchSongsOnly').attr("checked") === "checked")
                            return;

                        if (jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_chbSearchDAMOnly').attr("checked") === "checked")
                            return;

                        var data = "'prefixText':'" + prefixText + "', 'languageId': 1033, 'substoreIds': 'AllWebsites', 'renderOrderButton': true";
                        var categoryId = jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_ddlCategories').val();
                        if (categoryId) {
                            data += ", 'categoryId': '" + categoryId + "'";
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            data += ", 'categoryId': ''";
                        }

                        getSuggestionsRequest = jQuery.ajax({
                            type: "POST",
                            contentType: "application/json",
                            url: "/shop/AutoComplete.asmx/GetProductSuggestionsHtml",
                            data: "{" + data + "}",
                        dataType: "json",
                        async: true,
                        success: function (data) {
                            var $resultsPanel = jQuery('#resultsPanel');
                            $resultsPanel.html(data.d);
                            $resultsPanel.show();
                        },
                        error: function (result) {
                        }
                    });
                }

                var handleKey = function (sender, event) {
                    if (getSuggestionsRequest != null) {
                        getSuggestionsRequest.abort();
                    }

                    if (getSuggestionsTimer != null) {
                        clearTimeout(getSuggestionsTimer);
                    }

                    var prefixText = jQuery(sender).val();

                    getSuggestionsTimer = setTimeout(function () { updateSuggestionList(prefixText) }, 200);
                };

                var handleArrowsKey = function (sender, event) {
                    event.preventDefault();

                    var keyCode = event.keyCode;
                    if (keyCode != 38 && keyCode != 40)
                        return;

                    var $resTable = jQuery('#resultsPanel table.js-productListTable');
                    var $currectSelection = jQuery('tr.selected', $resTable);

                    var $elementToSelect = null;

                    if (keyCode == 40) {
                        if ($currectSelection.length == 0 || $currectSelection.next().length == 0)
                            $elementToSelect = jQuery('tr', $resTable).first();
                        else
                            $elementToSelect = $currectSelection.next();
                    }
                    else {
                        if ($currectSelection.length == 0 || $currectSelection.prev().length == 0)
                            $elementToSelect = jQuery('tr', $resTable).last();
                        else
                            $elementToSelect = $currectSelection.prev();
                    }

                    $currectSelection.removeClass('selected');
                    $elementToSelect.addClass('selected');
                }

                var handleEnterKey = function (sender, event) {
                    var $resTable = jQuery('#resultsPanel table.js-productListTable');
                    var $currectSelection = jQuery('tr.selected', $resTable);

                    if ($currectSelection.length == 0) {
                        jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_SearchButton').click();
                        return;
                    }

                    var productUrl = jQuery('#hrProductUrl', $currectSelection).val();
                    document.location = productUrl;
                }

                jQuery("#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_TopSearchBox").keyup(
	                function (event) {
	                    if (event.keyCode == 13) {
	                        handleEnterKey(this, event);
	                    } 
	                    else {
	                        handleKey(this, event);
	                    }
	                });

                jQuery(document).click(function (event) {
                    if (jQuery(event.target).parents('.controlBaseSearchContainer').length == 0)
                        jQuery('#resultsPanel').hide();
                });

                jQuery('.tbxSearch, #resultsPanel').click(function (e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                });
            });

            function SearchControl_AddToBasket(productId) {
                jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_hfSearchCurrentProductId').val(productId);
                jQuery('#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_btnSearchQuickOrder').click();

                if (typeof Sana == "object" && Sana.Header.isMobileLayout() && jQuery("#basketPage").length) {
                    $("#resultsPanel").hide();
                };

                setTimeout(function () {
                    jQuery("#ctl00_ctl00_ctl00_SiteSearchBar_CanViewCatalogSwitchPanel3_TopSearchBar_TopSearchBox").focus();
                }, 100);
            };



        </script>

        <!--
